Because the problem lies outside our codebase, we're unable to address it directly at the moment, but it will be addressed in the future by an NDK 29 update.\r\n\r\nWhen Writer\u003cTestStruct\u003e.write = DebugStruct is assigned in C#, IL2CPP stores it as an open static delegate. The Action_2_Invoke system routes through a generated OpenStatic trampoline. In NDK 27, the compiler incorrectly reordered a dependent store-then-load pair.\r\n\r\n- Option 1: Pass by Reference: Change the delegate to use ref T. This avoids large-struct register splitting. delegate void RefAction\u003cA, B\u003e(A a, ref B b);\r\n- Option 2: Use Reference Types Wrap the large internal State struct in a class. This shrinks the parent struct size to 8 bytes, keeping it in registers and avoiding the buggy stack-split path.\r\n- Option 3: Avoid Generic Delegate Trampolines Call the method directly (DebugStruct(writer, item)) rather than through a generic Action\u003cT\u003e field.\r\n"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"67:[\"$\",\"li\",\"17278\",{\"id\":\"issue-17278\",\"children\":[\"$\",\"$L4e\",null,{\"issue\":{\"description\":\"$78\",\"category\":{\"name\":\"Android\",\"product\":{\"displayName\":\"Unity\",\"packageName\":null,\"type\":\"UnityMain\"}},\"isCrash\":false,\"currentUser\":{\"hasVoted\":false,\"isWatching\":false},\"voteCount\":33,\"issuePorts\":[{\"streamName\":\"6000.6.X\",\"status\":9,\"state\":102,\"firstAffectedVersion\":null,\"resolutionNotes\":\"$79\",\"fixedInVersion\":null,\"isReleasedFixedInVersion\":null,\"lastUpdatedUtc\":\"2026-03-09T12:54:40.89\",\"streamDisplayName\":\"6.6.X\"},{\"streamName\":\"6000.5.X\",\"status\":9,\"state\":102,\"firstAffectedVersion\":null,\"resolutionNotes\":null,\"fixedInVersion\":null,\"isReleasedFixedInVersion\":null,\"lastUpdatedUtc\":\"2026-03-09T12:54:40.89\",\"streamDisplayName\":\"6.5.X\"},{\"streamName\":\"6000.4.X\",\"status\":9,\"state\":102,\"firstAffectedVersion\":null,\"resolutionNotes\":null,\"fixedInVersion\":null,\"isReleasedFixedInVersion\":null,\"lastUpdatedUtc\":\"2026-03-09T12:54:40.89\",\"streamDisplayName\":\"6.4.X\"},{\"streamName\":\"6000.3.X\",\"status\":9,\"state\":102,\"firstAffectedVersion\":null,\"resolutionNotes\":null,\"fixedInVersion\":null,\"isReleasedFixedInVersion\":null,\"lastUpdatedUtc\":\"2026-03-09T12:54:40.89\",\"streamDisplayName\":\"6.3.X\"},{\"streamName\":\"6000.0.X\",\"status\":9,\"state\":102,\"firstAffectedVersion\":\"6000.0.38f1\",\"resolutionNotes\":null,\"fixedInVersion\":null,\"isReleasedFixedInVersion\":null,\"lastUpdatedUtc\":\"2026-03-09T12:54:40.89\",\"streamDisplayName\":\"6.0.X\"}],\"discussionsUrl\":\"https://discussions.unity.com/t/1728359\",\"jiraUrlForEmployees\":\"https://jira.unity3d.com/browse/UUM-134192\",\"issueTrackerLink\":\"\",\"isDuplicate\":false,\"duplicatedIssueId\":null,\"id\":17278,\"externalId\":\"9772668\",\"externalKey\":\"UUM-134192\",\"title\":\"Struct
